# Dubon 2026 — reproduction manifest

Actual full adelic cyclic L2 unitarity: Seven further production modules prove that the original full adelic right-regular action preserves the genuine quotient L2 inner product and norm of every original algebraic cyclic vector. Positive real/finite matrices descend exactly to the already proved projective right action. The literal rational reflection acts by negating the two real off-diagonal entries and by finite projective conjugation. Its real continuous involution preserves the actual real Haar measure; the finite inner conjugation preserves the genuine finite Haar measure. Determinant-root normalization commutes with reflection, so the original rational arithmetic subgroup is preserved. The reflected domain is a genuine arithmetic fundamental domain, giving reflection invariance of quotient integrals. Original rational left invariance identifies right reflection with this actual projective involution. The original matrix sign decomposition then covers every full adelic matrix, including the negative real component, with no unitarity premise. ### Actual normalized Rankin continuation and positive Petersson residue — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Coverage: 432 production modules, 178 exact consumers, 2,132 explicit registrations and 3,793 exhaustive theorem dependency checks. Sixteen linters passed across 2,556 declarations plus 1,742 generated declarations in the audit scope; focused consumer lint had 1,737 generated declarations. Source/inventory and tooling fixtures passed: 539 retained files, 370 local links, 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files.

Seven modules justify the finite Möbius lattice assembly under the genuine cusp integral and its exact Petersson residue. Division by the actual principal-character completion factor gives the primitive Eisenstein cusp continuation. The proved real unfolding identity extends to every Re(s)>1 by the analytic identity theorem on the connected half-plane, using actual real-axis values accumulating at 2. Removing (4*pi)^(-(s+k-1))*Gamma(s+k-1) constructs a continuation of the literal normalized coefficient square series. At positive integral weight it is holomorphic for Re(s)>1/2 away from 1. For every nonzero cusp form, its residue is the strictly positive real constant gamma0EisensteinResidue(Q)*Petersson(f,f).re/cuspRankinFactor(k,1).re. Positivity follows from the genuine positive-definite pairing and the positive Gamma value; neither continuation nor a pole coefficient is assumed. Four exact consumers expose the literal primitive-row integral, coefficient sum, continuation and positive Petersson formula. This does not establish the quantitative Rankin–Selberg main term with x^(3/5) error. DUB-01–15 remain accepted; DUB-16–20 remain OPEN.

### Actual completed Rankin convolution and polynomial strip growth — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Coverage: 440 production modules, 183 exact consumers, 2,175 explicit registrations and 3,854 exhaustive theorem dependency checks. Sixteen linters passed across 2,605 declarations plus 1,760 generated declarations in the audit scope; focused consumer lint had 1,755 generated declarations. Source/inventory and tooling fixtures passed: 547 retained files, 378 local links, 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files.

Eight modules prove the actual integrated rectangular lattice reflection, an entire completion after removing both poles, and a quadratic bound on every closed vertical strip. The bound uses the genuine convergent cusp integral majorant, finite Möbius coefficients and positive real powers. The square-supported principal-character coefficient function is constructed by an injective square lift. Its actual Dirichlet series is proved equal to L(2s,chi_0). The genuine convolution with normalized cusp coefficient squares is a finite divisor-antidiagonal sum; each coefficient is real and nonnegative. Pinned Mathlib's L-series convolution theorem gives the exact product and the precise two-Gamma completion. At full level the completed convolution has the scalar s-to-1-s functional equation. At general levels the proved reflection is a finite rectangular lattice identity; no scalar functional equation is claimed there. Five exact consumers expose the literal integrals, coefficients, exact completion, strip bound and full-level reflection. These facts do not give the quantitative x^(3/5) summatory error. DUB-01–15 remain accepted; DUB-16–20 remain OPEN.

Ivić's historical full-level overview was rechecked on 7 October at https://arxiv.org/pdf/math/0603013, Sections 1–3: the convolution and degree-four functional equation guide the next quantitative step. Its source estimate is not used as a Lean hypothesis or treated as current optimality evidence. No new frozen artifact or dependency upgrade was introduced.

### Actual analytic Tauberian inversion — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Coverage: 458 production modules, 190 exact consumers, 2,251 explicit registrations and 4,022 exhaustive theorem dependency checks. Sixteen linters passed across 2,690 declarations plus 1,852 generated declarations in the audit scope; focused consumer lint had 1,847 generated declarations. Source/inventory and tooling fixtures passed: 565 retained files, 411 local links, 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files.

Nine modules prove the analytic Tauberian theorem for the genuine Laplace transform of a bounded measurable function. They establish actual full/truncated integrability, entire truncated dependence, exact rectangular Cauchy identities, kernel cancellation at the imaginary endpoints, both 12B/R half-contour bounds and dominated convergence on the fixed left contour. The resulting theorem proves convergence of the actual improper integral to the continued transform at zero. Its exact consumer displays the literal Laplace integral hypothesis. This is an auxiliary analytic result: application to the actual cusp summatory error and the central weighted formula are not yet registered or verified. Exact x^(3/5), Deligne and unconditional Sato–Tate remain open. DUB-01–15 remain accepted; DUB-16–20 remain OPEN.

### Actual Gamma amplitudes and literal Riesz block estimates — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Coverage: 503 production modules, 202 exact consumers, 2,450 explicit registrations and 4,382 exhaustive theorem dependency checks. Sixteen linters passed across 2,927 declarations plus 2,013 generated declarations in the audit scope; focused consumer lint had 2,008 generated declarations. Source/inventory and tooling fixtures passed: 610 retained files, 510 local links, 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files.

Ten modules prove actual horizontal Gamma log-modulus and digamma variation estimates, the sharp reflected-ratio amplitude power and derivative bounds, and actual integration by parts. The complete two-ratio Gamma product has uniform dyadic integral bounds and inverse-square-root block decay outside the stationary logarithmic window. The literal Riesz Gamma symbol consumes all shift positivity, exponent, frequency and curvature bridges on beta=3/8-r/4, 0<=r<=2. Thus the actual finite-interval Riesz integrands have the exact x^(-1/8) and x^(3/8) powers at orders two and zero. No improper convergence or full-kernel bound is asserted at this milestone. Those limits and bounds, kernel differentiation, contour shifting, dual sums and the exact x^(3/5) remainder remain open. General level still requires its genuine dual-coefficient/Fricke bridge; Deligne and unconditional Sato–Tate remain open. DUB-01–15 remain accepted; DUB-16–20 remain OPEN.

### Actual improper Riesz Gamma kernels and uniform sharp powers — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Coverage: 510 production modules, 204 exact consumers, 2,480 explicit registrations and 4,422 exhaustive theorem dependency checks. Sixteen linters passed across 2,963 declarations plus 2,023 generated declarations in the audit scope; focused consumer lint had 2,018 generated declarations. Source/inventory and tooling fixtures passed: 617 retained files, 527 local links, 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files.

Seven modules prove integrable t^(-3/2) decay of the actual amplitude derivative, full-interval nonstationary bounds for both frequency signs, a genuine real-indexed Cauchy limit for the positive-half integral, and the exact conjugate-symmetric improper normalization. A proved stationary window, two actual dyadic blocks and the fixed compact Gamma amplitude integral yield a uniform fixed-parameter bound C_(k,r) x^(3/8-r/4) for every x>0, 0<=r<=2 and k>=2. The exact order-two and order-zero powers are x^(-1/8) and x^(3/8). Two literal consumers expose both the finite-cutoff limit and these exact powers. No whole-line Bochner integral replaces the conditionally convergent integral. Kernel derivative identities, the actual functional-equation contour transform, polynomial strip bounds, dual sums and the exact x^(3/5) remainder remain open, as do the general-level dual-coefficient/Fricke bridge, Deligne and unconditional Sato–Tate. DUB-01–15 remain accepted; DUB-16–20 remain OPEN.

### Actual Riesz Gamma contour shift and compact uniform convergence — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Coverage: 517 production modules, 206 exact consumers, 2,504 explicit registrations and 4,456 exhaustive theorem dependency checks. Sixteen linters passed across 2,990 declarations plus 2,033 generated declarations in the audit scope; focused consumer lint had 2,028 generated declarations. Source/inventory and tooling fixtures passed: 624 retained files, 544 local links, 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files.

Seven modules prove inverse-square-root cutoff errors, compact uniform convergence of the actual critical-line integrals, holomorphy of the genuine Mellin integrand on the required strip, uniform Gamma amplitude bounds across that strip, and vanishing of both actual horizontal edges. The exact Cauchy rectangle identity shifts every critical Riesz line to any line up to Re(s)=3/8 without changing the actual improper kernel; this convergence is also uniform on each closed positive x interval. Two literal consumers expose the actual truncation error and shifted-contour uniform limit. This is a Gamma-kernel contour shift, not the Dirichlet-series functional-equation contour transform. Kernel derivative identities, the latter transform, polynomial strip bounds, dual sums and the exact x^(3/5) remainder remain open, as do the general-level dual-coefficient/Fricke bridge, Deligne and unconditional Sato–Tate. DUB-01–15 remain accepted; DUB-16–20 remain OPEN.

### Actual Riesz kernel derivatives and sharp second differences — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Coverage: 525 production modules, 208 exact consumers, 2,521 explicit registrations and 4,484 exhaustive theorem dependency checks. Sixteen linters passed across 3,009 declarations plus 2,044 generated declarations in the audit scope; focused consumer lint had 2,039 generated declarations. Source/inventory and tooling fixtures passed: 632 retained files, 562 local links, 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files.

Eight modules prove the actual Gamma recurrence and weighted-integrand derivatives, compact joint continuity and genuine local domination for finite parameter differentiation, weighted finite-cutoff normalization and derivatives, and compact-uniform convergence after positive dilation. The derivative-limit theorem yields (x K_1(c x)) prime = K_0(c x) and (x^2 K_2(c x)) prime = x K_1(c x), for every positive c,x. Two actual mean-value inequalities combined with the sharp order-zero bound give the low-frequency second-difference bound C_k h^2 (c x)^(3/8). The genuine order-two bound at all three cutoffs gives the high-frequency bound C_k c^(-1/8) x^(15/8), for 0<=h<=x. Two exact consumers expose the derivatives and literal three-point difference. The Rankin Dirichlet-series functional-equation transform, residues, polynomial strip bounds, justified dual-series interchange, coefficient power sums and exact x^(3/5) remain open. General-level dual-coefficient/Fricke input, Deligne and unconditional Sato–Tate also remain open. DUB-01–15 remain accepted; DUB-16–20 remain OPEN.

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Four modules prove exact Abel formulas for genuine prefix and finite-tail sums and derive real cumulative bounds from the natural linear mean. Exact power integrals give the sharp prefix growth and finite-tail decay. Arbitrary finite-subset comparison proves actual infinite-tail summability and its bound. The genuine Rankin convolution coefficients therefore have the precise n^(-5/8) prefix bound C_f N^(3/8) and convergent n^(-9/8) tail bound C_f N^(-1/8), for every N>=1. The exact source consumer takes the actual cusp form and positive weight, with neither a mean nor tail convergence assumed by the caller. The actual dual Gamma series, its justified interchange with the Rankin functional-equation contour transform, pole residues, polynomial strip bounds and exact x^(3/5) remain open. General-level dual-coefficient/Fricke input, Deligne and unconditional Sato–Tate also remain open. DUB-01–15 remain accepted; DUB-16–20 remain OPEN.

### Actual rescaled cusp traces and common-period Mellin identity — 7 October 2026

The original product-level cusp form is translated by the actual inverse diagonal matrix. The mixed subgroup has exactly the upper-right divisibility by a and lower-left divisibility by b; its real image is proved equal to the diagonal conjugate of Gamma0(ab), and it contains Gamma(ab). Restricting each genuine integral coset translate to the normal principal subgroup therefore supplies one common period ab. The finite sum retains every actual coset once. Its Petersson density is full-level invariant, so its horizontal square energy is one-periodic. Exact averaging over ab periods and actual Parseval identify that unit-interval integral with the complete finite family of q-expansion squares.

Every actual normalized period coefficient family has a proved linear mean. Summing the genuine finite family and convolving with the full-level principal square sequence preserves nonnegativity and the linear mean, giving absolute convergence for Re(s)>1. The exact Mellin transform has factor (4 pi / ab)^(-(s+k-1)) Gamma(s+k-1). Absolute Bochner integrability is proved by summable integral norms; no formal exchange of a divergent integral and series is used. The integer and projective coset domains have equal multiplicities because the mixed group absorbs the center, and diagonal transport of the original Gamma0 domain gives a second genuine domain for the same real projective group.

This is the actual coefficient and domain construction. The reflected lattice-cusp integral has not yet been identified with this coefficient series. That identification still needs the invariant completed-Eisenstein trace unfolding and the exact determinant/conductor factors. The general-level contour estimates and x^(3/5) conclusion remain OPEN. The already verified full-level Rankin–Selberg theorem and all weighted energy regimes remain intact. Deligne and unconditional Sato–Tate remain OPEN. No gate changes: 15/20 accepted.

### Actual Sato–Tate character and prime-average bridge — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Coverage: 611 production modules, 236 exact consumers, 2,867 explicit registrations and 5,078 exhaustive theorem dependency checks. All sixteen linters passed across 3,400 declarations plus 2,288 generated declarations in both audit executions. Initial inventory passed with 718 retained files, 1,051 local links and 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files; source pins and tooling fixtures passed.

Seven modules establish exact character orthogonality for the genuine Sato–Tate coefficient law, polynomial density and actual weak probability convergence, the normalized primitive prime-power Hecke recurrence, the precise finite empirical integral, and vanishing errors from the actual finite ramified prime set. The final primitive-form consumer takes only two explicitly displayed arithmetic inputs: the good-prime norm bound and vanishing positive-order prime-power averages. It discharges real normalization, all finite ramified terms, weak convergence, positive-density selection and compact-uniform isolated-prime H2. Neither arithmetic premise has yet been proved. This is a verified conditional analytic/normalization bridge; DUB-17–20 remain OPEN. DUB-01–16 remain accepted.

A focused build initially reported a deprecated probability-mass lemma. It was replaced with `probReal_univ`; subsequent focused compilation and both complete verifiers passed with zero diagnostics. Three existing module headers now accurately distinguish the separately proved general-level Rankin–Selberg input from the still-unproved Sato–Tate input. No theorem or source hypothesis was weakened.

### Actual local logarithmic derivatives and higher-prime-power correction verification — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Initial inventory passed with 734 retained files, 1,164 local links and 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files; source pins and tooling fixtures passed.

Five modules and three exact consumers prove the genuine local spectral Euler logarithmic derivatives, their exact first-prime/higher-power decomposition, and the actual remainder's holomorphy on Re(s)>1/2. A uniform prime majorant log(p)p^(-2a) is summable by actual von Mangoldt convergence. Primitive Hecke roots supply the original symmetric-power trace coefficients and exact local Euler factors, with the finite ramified set explicitly removed. The original prime-character L-series is proved to be the genuine local derivative series minus that analytic remainder. Its boundary continuation therefore follows from continuation of the local derivative series, and the already proved Tauberian/PNT/character chain yields weak Sato--Tate and full H2.

The actual local coefficient bound and boundary continuation of the derivative series remain explicit unproved arithmetic inputs. The genuine global spectral product/logarithmic-derivative identification, automorphic and Galois constructions, Deligne purity and required global continuation/nonvanishing remain open. DUB-17--20 remain OPEN and DUB-01--16 remain accepted. Focused production/source checks and both full verifiers passed with zero diagnostics; no target hypothesis was weakened.

Eight modules and four exact consumers prove quantitative bounds for both actual Satake roots, identify the real nonnegative power traces of the genuine Rankin tensor roots, and establish the finite logarithmic and global three-four-one Euler inequalities. A zero at one would cancel the two principal poles through the actual divided difference, producing an entire nonnegative-coefficient Dirichlet series with a genuine trivial zero at -2, which contradicts proved Dirichlet positivity. Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. ### Genuine projective adelic Haar measure verification — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Coverage: 773 production modules, 332 exact consumers, 3,707 explicit registrations and 6,562 exhaustive theorem dependency checks. All sixteen linters passed across 4,445 declarations plus 2,910 generated declarations in both paper audit executions. Initial inventory passed with 880 retained files, 1,577 local links and 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files; source pins and tooling fixtures passed.

Four modules and two exact consumers construct the actual locally compact Hausdorff finite projective general-linear group, compact open level images and genuine Haar measure normalized on the original full-level subgroup. Rational strong approximation proves a countable compact cover and sigma-finiteness. Literal reflection and coordinate-swap identities, actual determinant factorization and finite adelic SL2 elementary generation prove that every original nonnegative character of the finite projective group is trivial. Consequently the actual modular character is one and the normalized Haar measure is right invariant. The existing real projective Haar measure and this finite measure give the genuine product Haar measure, invariant on both sides. The literal real full-level region times finite full-level compact subgroup has exactly the existing finite real volume.

Descent of the original cyclic functions to these projective coordinates, the rational arithmetic fundamental-domain theorem, invariant adelic L2 pairing, completed adelic cuspidal representation, irreducibility and higher symmetric continuation remain open. The finite-volume product region is not yet claimed as an adelic arithmetic fundamental domain. Unconditional Deligne, non-CM Sato–Tate and the final modular application remain open. DUB-01–16 remain accepted; DUB-17–20 remain OPEN.

### Actual adelic arithmetic fundamental domain and faithful L2 verification — 7 October 2026

Foundation `cmd /c run_lake_build.bat --no-pause` returned **PASS, exit 0**, followed sequentially by paper `cmd /c run_dubon_build.bat --no-pause`, **DEVELOPMENT PASS, exit 0**, with zero Lean diagnostics. Coverage: 784 production modules, 340 exact consumers, 3,765 explicit registrations and 6,659 exhaustive theorem dependency checks. All sixteen linters passed across 4,521 declarations plus 2,947 generated declarations in both paper audit executions. Initial inventory passed with 891 retained files, 1,610 local links and 4,320 unchanged existing Lean files; source pins and tooling fixtures passed.

Eight further production modules construct the actual positive-rational arithmetic image in PSL2(R) times PGL2(A_f), prove invariance of every original cyclic vector, and identify the finite-level intersection exactly with the faithfully embedded original integral Gamma0 group. The literal real Gamma0 fundamental domain times the genuine compact finite level subgroup is proved to be a fundamental domain for the same full arithmetic group at every nonzero level. Its Haar volume is exactly the product of the real-domain and finite-level volumes, with the latter positive and finite. Countable rational strong approximation supplies almost-everywhere coverage; the exact intersection and original real tiling give null overlaps. The original real cusp bound proves global boundedness of every full adelic cyclic vector, hence genuine square integrability on the actual domain. Quotient integrals and the actual cyclic pairing are invariant under every right projective translation. The original algebraic full adelic cyclic space embeds complex-linearly and injectively into actual L2, whose inner product is precisely the original integral pairing. Unitarity under the original full adelic right representation, including its negative-real action, the completed adelic cuspidal representation, irreducibility and higher symmetric continuation remain open. Unconditional Deligne, non-CM Sato–Tate and the final modular application remain open. DUB-01–16 remain accepted; DUB-17–20 remain OPEN.
